#### Product Information

###### Product Overview

### Gradient Collection Waterproof Click Lock Luxury Vinyl Plank Flooring

Create a clean, contemporary foundation for your home with the Gradient Collection. Designed to capture the natural beauty of hardwood while delivering the performance advantages of modern vinyl flooring, these waterproof luxury vinyl planks feature realistic wood visuals, a durable wear layer, and a rigid WPC core that stands up to everyday life. The result is a stylish, low-maintenance floor that performs beautifully in busy households and light commercial settings alike.

#### Stylish Wood-Look Flooring for Everyday Living

The Gradient Collection is ideal for homeowners seeking the warmth and character of hardwood without the upkeep. Its realistic grain patterns and natural color variation bring depth and visual interest to any room, while the waterproof construction provides confidence in areas where spills, moisture, and daily activity are common.

From modern open-concept spaces to traditional interiors, this flooring offers a versatile design that complements a wide range of décor styles.

#### Built for Long-Term Durability

Engineered with an 8.5 mm overall thickness and a rigid waterproof WPC core, the Gradient Collection delivers excellent dimensional stability and everyday resilience. A durable 20 mil wear layer helps protect against scratches, scuffs, stains, and wear caused by foot traffic, pets, and active families.

The embossed-in-register (EIR) texture closely follows the printed wood grain for a more authentic hardwood appearance and feel. Combined with a low-sheen matte finish, the planks create a realistic and sophisticated look throughout the home.

FloorScore® certification supports healthier indoor air quality, while residential and commercial warranty coverage provide added peace of mind.

#### Fast and Convenient Installation

Featuring the trusted Unilin Uniclic click-lock system, the Gradient Collection installs as a floating floor without the need for glue, staples, or nails. The precision-milled locking mechanism helps create tight seams and a secure fit for a professional-looking result.

An attached 1.5 mm IXPE underlayment enhances comfort underfoot while helping reduce noise transfer between rooms. This integrated pad also simplifies installation by eliminating the need for a separate underlayment in many applications.

#### Where This Product Works Best

- Kitchens and dining rooms
- Bathrooms and laundry areas
- Basements and lower-level living spaces
- Living rooms and family rooms
- Hallways, foyers, and entryways
- Rental properties and multifamily housing
- Homes with children and pets
- Light commercial environments
- Whole-home flooring projects

#### Quick Specs Summary

- Flooring Type: Luxury Vinyl Plank (LVP)
- Core Construction: Waterproof WPC Core
- Thickness: 8.5 mm
- Attached Underlayment: 1.5 mm IXPE Pad
- Wear Layer: 20 mil
- Installation Method: Unilin Uniclic Click Lock
- Edge Profile: Micro Beveled
- Surface Finish: Matte
- Texture: Embossed in Register (EIR)
- Waterproof: Yes
- Installation Grade: Above, On, or Below Grade
- Indoor Air Quality: FloorScore® Certified
- Residential Warranty: 25 Years
- Commercial Warranty: 15 Years

#### A Practical Choice for Beautiful, Low-Maintenance Floors

The Gradient Collection combines the authentic appearance of hardwood with the durability, waterproof protection, and installation simplicity that modern homeowners demand. Its rigid core construction, integrated underpad, and commercial-grade wear layer make it an excellent option for high-traffic homes, renovation projects, and spaces where moisture resistance is essential.

Whether you're refreshing a single room or upgrading an entire home, this collection delivers lasting performance and timeless style.

###### Benefits

- 100% waterproof WPC core construction
- Durable 20 mil wear layer for everyday protection
- Realistic wood visuals with embossed-in-register texture
- Attached 1.5 mm IXPE underpad for comfort and noise reduction
- Easy Unilin Uniclic click-lock installation
- Suitable for above-, on-, and below-grade applications
- FloorScore® certified for indoor air quality
- Matte finish for a natural hardwood look
- Excellent choice for kitchens, bathrooms, basements, and busy living spaces
- Backed by 25-year residential and 15-year commercial warranties

## Page 4 — FanFold XPS Flooring Underlayment | 100 Sq Ft | BuildDirect

**🔗 URL:** https://www.builddirect.com/products/fanfold-underlayment

> XPS fanfold underlayment for laminate & engineered hardwood. Moisture barrier, 72 IIC sound rating, radiant heat compatible. Covers 100 sq ft fast.

Order up to 5 Free Samples Today

Take Our Quiz To Find Your Perfect Floor →

- Home
- FanFold XPS Flooring Underlayment – 100 sq. ft. (2mm)

Share:

#### Product Information

###### Product Overview

#### FanFold XPS Flooring Underlayment for Laminate & Engineered Hardwood

Cover more floor in less time. This fanfold XPS underlayment unfolds into wide 46.7" panels that cover 100 square feet per box — fewer seams, less taping, and faster prep than rolled foam. The rigid extruded polystyrene (XPS) core smooths minor subfloor imperfections and creates a firm, stable base for floating laminate and engineered hardwood floors.

##### Built-In Moisture Protection

The closed-cell XPS construction acts as a moisture barrier between your subfloor and finished floor — a must over concrete slabs, basements, and ground-level installs. Skip the separate vapor film and protect your floor's locking system from subfloor moisture in one step.

##### Quieter Floors, Warmer Rooms

With a 72 IIC / 67 STC sound rating and a 22 dB Delta IIC, this underlayment cuts the hollow sound and footstep noise common with floating floors — a strong choice for upper levels, condos, and multi-family projects with sound requirements. The XPS core also delivers an R-value of 5.0 per inch, adding a thermal break that keeps floors warmer over concrete.

##### Radiant Heat Ready

Compatible with both electric and hydronic radiant heat systems, so you can pair it with heated floors without compromising performance.

##### Fast, Pro-Friendly Installation

Panels lay flat right out of the box, cut cleanly with a utility knife, and tape at the seams. No adhesives, no special tools. One box, one installer, one room knocked out before lunch.

Where it works best:

- Floating laminate and engineered hardwood floors
- Concrete, plywood, and OSB subfloors
- Basements, condos, and multi-story homes where sound and moisture matter
- Radiant heat installations (electric or hydronic)

Not for use with: flooring that has pre-attached underlayment pad.

Backed by a 25-year residential and 5-year commercial warranty.

###### Benefits

- Fanfold design covers large areas quickly
- Helps smooth minor subfloor imperfections
- Lightweight and easy to cut and handle
- Supports quieter, more comfortable floors
- Cost-effective solution for floating floor installations

#### Product Information

###### Product Overview

#### Westport Collection Engineered White Oak Hardwood Flooring

The Westport Collection engineered hardwood flooring combines the timeless beauty of European white oak with a clean, modern wide-plank design. Its wire-brushed surface and low-gloss finish highlight the natural grain and variation of real oak while helping soften the look of everyday wear. The result is a bright, relaxed hardwood floor that works beautifully in both contemporary and traditional interiors.

With its engineered construction and real wood surface, Westport delivers the warmth and authenticity of hardwood while offering reliable performance for modern homes.

##### Designed for Everyday Living

Westport engineered hardwood is designed for residential spaces where homeowners want the natural character of real wood with practical durability. The wide planks and light oak tones help create an open, airy feel, making it a strong choice for living rooms, bedrooms, and open-concept spaces.

It’s a popular option for whole-home flooring updates where consistent style and long-term performance are important.

##### Built for Performance and Quality

This flooring features a real European white oak wear layer supported by a durable engineered core. The wire-brushed texture enhances the wood’s natural character while helping reduce the visibility of small scratches and everyday scuffs.

A protective low-gloss urethane finish helps guard against daily wear while maintaining a natural wood appearance. The floor is also FloorScore certified, meaning it meets strict indoor air quality standards for low VOC emissions which is an important consideration for healthier indoor environments.

##### Installation Made Easier

Westport flooring uses a traditional tongue-and-groove profile that supports glue-down, staple, or floating installation depending on the project requirements. Because the boards arrive prefinished, installation is faster and cleaner compared to site-finished hardwood.

Long average board lengths create a more continuous visual across the floor, while subtle microbevel edges add plank definition without overpowering the space.

##### Ideal Rooms and Applications

- Living rooms and family rooms
- Bedrooms and dining areas
- Hallways and open-concept main floors
- Homes with radiant heat systems

- Warranty: Lifetime residential warranty

##### A Smart Choice for Modern Hardwood Floors

The Westport Collection offers a balanced combination of natural hardwood beauty and practical performance. Wide planks and authentic white oak grain create a high-end visual, while engineered construction provides reliable performance in everyday residential settings. With FloorScore certification for low VOC emissions and a lifetime residential warranty, this collection delivers added confidence for homeowners investing in real hardwood flooring.

#### Product Information

###### Product Overview

### Artisan Brushed Oak Engineered Hardwood Collection

The Artisan Brushed Oak Engineered Hardwood Collection brings natural warmth and handcrafted character into your home. Each plank features a wire-brushed surface that enhances the natural oak grain, creating subtle texture and depth that feels both refined and relaxed. The result is a hardwood floor that looks beautifully natural while adding durability for everyday living.

With its wide plank format and authentic wood character, this engineered hardwood flooring complements a wide range of interior styles, from modern and transitional spaces to rustic and farmhouse designs.

#### Ideal for Beautiful, Everyday Living Spaces

Engineered hardwood flooring is designed to deliver the classic look of real wood with improved structural stability. The Artisan Brushed Oak collection is perfect for homeowners who want genuine hardwood beauty in high-visibility living spaces.

These floors are commonly used in living rooms, bedrooms, dining areas, and home offices, as well as open-concept main floors where a consistent hardwood look ties the space together. The engineered construction helps it perform reliably in homes with seasonal humidity changes.

#### Durable Construction Built for Long-Term Performance

The wire-brushed surface treatment highlights the natural oak grain while helping mask minor scratches, scuffs, and daily wear. This makes it a practical choice for busy households where style and durability are equally important.

Underneath the real oak veneer, a multi-layer engineered core provides enhanced dimensional stability compared to solid hardwood. This layered design helps minimize movement caused by humidity and temperature fluctuations, supporting long-lasting performance. A protective finish seals the surface while maintaining the organic texture and character of real oak.

#### Designed for a Smooth Installation Experience

The Artisan Brushed Oak planks are precision-milled to ensure tight seams and a consistent, professional finish once installed. Engineered hardwood offers flexibility during installation, making it suitable for a variety of project types.

Depending on your subfloor and installation preference, these planks can typically be installed using floating, glue-down, or nail-down methods. They can also be installed over common subfloors such as plywood, OSB, or properly prepared concrete.

#### Where This Flooring Works Best

- Living rooms and family rooms
- Bedrooms and home offices
- Dining areas and open-concept layouts
- Renovation projects where solid hardwood may not be ideal
- Homes with moderate seasonal humidity changes
- Rustic, farmhouse, modern, and transitional interior designs

- Wood Species: Oak

- Plank Style: Wide plank hardwood flooring
- Installation Methods: Floating, glue-down, or nail-down
- Grade: Character-rich natural oak appearance

#### A Smart Choice for Natural Hardwood Style

The Artisan Brushed Oak Engineered Hardwood Collection combines the authentic beauty of oak with the stability and versatility of engineered construction. Its textured surface enhances the natural wood grain while helping maintain a beautiful appearance in everyday living environments.

For homeowners seeking timeless hardwood character with practical performance, this collection offers a balanced, dependable flooring solution.

#### Product Information

###### Product Overview

### Influence Collection Waterproof Flooring

Upgrade your space with the Influence Collection 5.5mm SPC click lock vinyl planks, a durable, waterproof flooring solution designed for modern living. With a rigid stone polymer core and realistic wood-look finishes, this vinyl plank flooring delivers the perfect balance of style, strength, and everyday practicality.

Built for both residential and light commercial use, these planks are engineered to handle high traffic while maintaining their appearance over time.

#### Designed for Everyday Living

This SPC vinyl flooring is ideal for homeowners and professionals looking for a low-maintenance, high-performance alternative to hardwood. It brings a warm, natural wood look into living rooms and bedrooms, while also performing exceptionally well in kitchens and bathrooms where moisture resistance is essential. It’s a reliable option for basements and condos thanks to its structural stability, and a practical choice for rental properties or commercial spaces that require long-lasting durability. The click lock system also makes it especially appealing for DIY installations and fast renovations.

#### Strong, Stable, and Waterproof

At the core of this flooring is a stone polymer composite (SPC), which provides exceptional dimensional stability and resistance to temperature fluctuations. Unlike traditional vinyl or laminate, it won’t expand or contract easily, making it a dependable choice for areas with changing conditions.

The waterproof construction protects against spills, moisture, and everyday accidents, while the durable wear layer resists scratches, scuffs, and stains. This makes it especially well-suited for busy households, including those with pets.

#### Easy Installation Experience

The click lock system allows planks to securely snap together without the need for glue or nails, creating a straightforward floating floor installation. It can be installed over most existing subfloors with minimal preparation, helping to save time on the job.

The process requires only basic tools and is suitable for both DIYers and professionals. The rigid core construction also helps mask minor subfloor imperfections, while any attached underlayment can enhance comfort underfoot and improve sound absorption.

#### Where This Flooring Works Best

- High-traffic residential areas
- Kitchens, bathrooms, and laundry rooms
- Basements and below-grade installations
- Rental units and multi-family housing
- Light commercial environments

#### Quick Specs

- Thickness: 5.5mm total construction
- Core: SPC (Stone Polymer Composite) rigid core
- Installation: Click lock floating system
- Water Resistance: 100% waterproof
- Durability: Scratch and wear-resistant surface
- Style: Wood-look vinyl plank
- Maintenance: Low-maintenance, easy to clean

#### A Smart, Stylish Flooring Choice

The Influence Collection offers the look of hardwood with the performance of advanced vinyl technology. It’s an excellent option if you want flooring that can stand up to real life without sacrificing design. From busy households to demanding environments, it delivers consistent performance and long-term value.

- Realistic wood visuals enhance any space
- Easy click lock installation saves time and cost

#### Product Information

###### Product Overview

### Vigorous Collection 5.5mm SPC Click Lock Vinyl Planks

Upgrade your space with the Vigorous Collection 5.5mm SPC click lock vinyl planks, a practical, stylish flooring solution designed for everyday life. Built with a rigid stone polymer core, this waterproof vinyl plank flooring delivers the look of natural wood with the durability modern homes demand.

#### Designed for Everyday Performance

These SPC vinyl planks are designed to handle high-traffic areas in both residential and light commercial spaces. Whether you're renovating a busy household or updating a rental property, this flooring stands up to daily wear while maintaining a clean, contemporary appearance. The click lock system makes it ideal for floating floor installations without the need for glue or nails.

#### Durable, Stable, and Waterproof

The rigid core construction provides excellent dimensional stability, helping prevent expansion or contraction from temperature and humidity changes. A protective wear layer resists scratches, stains, and dents, making it a reliable option for homes with pets, kids, or heavy foot traffic. Its waterproof design also makes it suitable for moisture-prone areas like kitchens, bathrooms, and basements.

#### Simple Click Lock Installation

Designed with convenience in mind, the click lock system allows for fast, straightforward installation. The planks connect securely and precisely, reducing gaps and ensuring a smooth, professional-looking finish. Minimal subfloor preparation is required compared to traditional flooring options.

#### Ideal Spaces for Installation

- Living rooms and family rooms
- Kitchens and dining areas
- Bathrooms and laundry rooms
- Basements and lower levels
- Rental properties and light commercial spaces

#### Key Product Specifications

- Total thickness: 5.5mm
- Core: SPC (Stone Plastic Composite) rigid core
- Installation: Click lock floating system
- Water resistance: 100% waterproof
- Surface: Scratch and stain resistant wear layer
- Style: Wood-look vinyl planks
- Maintenance: Low-maintenance, easy to clean

#### A Reliable, Cost-Effective Flooring Solution

The Vigorous Collection offers a balance of durability, style, and affordability. It’s an excellent option for homeowners looking for a long-lasting floor that can handle daily wear without sacrificing visual appeal. With its waterproof core and easy installation, it simplifies both renovation and new build projects.

#### Slip Resistance

- Test method: ANSI A326.3-2017 (Dynamic Coefficient of Friction)
- Condition tested: Wet
- Result: Wet DCOF = 0.45

What this means:

A Wet DCOF of 0.45 meets the commonly referenced industry threshold (≥ 0.42) for level interior walking surfaces expected to be wet, indicating good slip resistance under wet conditions per ANSI A326.3-2017.

- Scratch and stain resistant surface ideal for busy homes
- Easy click lock installation—no glue or nails required
- Suitable for both residential and light commercial use

#### Product Information

###### Product Overview

### Celestial SPC Click Lock Waterproof Engineered Hardwood Flooring

Upgrade your space with the rich character of real hardwood and the added confidence of waterproof performance with the Celestial SPC Click Lock Collection from BuildDirect. This engineered hardwood flooring combines an authentic wood veneer surface with a rigid SPC core, delivering the warmth and texture of hardwood alongside enhanced moisture resistance and dimensional stability. The wide planks, modern low-gloss finish, and durable construction make this collection a practical choice for busy households and contemporary interiors.

#### Designed for Real-World Everyday Living

The Celestial SPC Click Lock Collection is built for homeowners who want the beauty of genuine hardwood without many of the limitations associated with traditional wood flooring. Its waterproof SPC (Stone Plastic Composite) core helps resist moisture-related expansion and contraction, making it suitable for areas where spills, humidity, and daily activity are part of normal life.

The real wood veneer layer preserves the natural grain variation and texture homeowners expect from hardwood flooring while the rigid core construction adds durability and a more stable feel underfoot. This combination makes it especially appealing for active households with children, pets, or frequent entertaining.

#### Waterproof Performance with Hardwood Style

Unlike traditional hardwood flooring, SPC engineered hardwood is designed to better handle moisture-prone residential environments. The waterproof core helps protect against spills and surface moisture while maintaining the visual appeal of authentic hardwood flooring. The click-lock installation system also creates tight plank connections that support cleaner, faster installations.

The durable finish layer helps resist scratches, scuffs, and everyday wear, while the low-maintenance surface simplifies regular cleaning. Wide plank styling and realistic wood visuals contribute to a premium, modern hardwood appearance suitable for both open-concept spaces and smaller rooms seeking a more expansive feel.

Homeowners comparing waterproof hardwood flooring options often prioritize SPC-core construction for its dimensional stability and easier maintenance compared to conventional solid hardwood. Flooring discussions frequently note that rigid-core engineered hardwood can offer a strong balance between authentic wood appearance and practical performance for family homes.

#### Easier Installation for Renovations and New Projects

The click-lock floating installation system is designed to simplify the installation process while reducing project time. Because the planks lock together mechanically, installation can often move more quickly than traditional glue-down hardwood applications. This makes the Celestial collection a strong option for DIY-capable homeowners and professional remodelers alike.

Its rigid SPC core can also help minimize minor subfloor imperfections while providing improved stability across varying indoor conditions. The prefinished planks eliminate the need for sanding or finishing on-site, helping keep installation cleaner and more convenient.

#### Where This Flooring Works Best

- Kitchens and dining areas
- Living rooms and open-concept spaces
- Basements and lower-level residential areas
- Bedrooms and hallways
- Homes with children or pets
- Renovation projects needing waterproof hardwood performance

The Celestial SPC Click Lock Collection offers a practical solution for homeowners who want authentic hardwood visuals with better moisture resistance and easier maintenance than traditional hardwood flooring. By combining a real wood surface with a waterproof SPC core, this collection delivers the warmth and character of wood flooring while helping reduce concerns about spills, humidity, and seasonal movement.

Its click-lock installation system, durable finish, and stable rigid-core construction make it especially well suited for modern households looking for a balance between style, convenience, and long-term performance. The result is a floor that delivers the visual appeal of hardwood with added confidence for everyday living.

### Andes Engineered Hardwood Flooring

Bring natural warmth and contemporary style into your home with Andes Engineered Hardwood Flooring. Designed with the authentic beauty of real hardwood and the added stability of engineered construction, this collection offers a sophisticated balance of performance, durability, and modern design. The wide planks, rich wood visuals, and low-sheen finish create a clean, upscale appearance that works beautifully across a variety of residential interiors.

Whether you are updating a single room or designing an entire home, the Andes Collection delivers the timeless appeal of hardwood with enhanced versatility for today’s living environments.

#### Designed for Modern Residential Spaces

The Andes Collection combines real hardwood veneer with a multi-layer engineered core to help improve dimensional stability in changing indoor conditions. This construction helps reduce the expansion and contraction commonly associated with solid hardwood, making it a practical choice for a wider range of installation areas within the home.

The natural wood grain and color variation create visual depth and character throughout the floor, while the smooth finish provides a refined, contemporary appearance that complements both traditional and modern interiors.

Wide planks help rooms feel more open and spacious while showcasing the natural movement and detail of the hardwood surface.

#### Reliable Everyday Performance

Engineered hardwood is valued for its ability to combine the look of solid wood with improved structural stability. The durable factory-applied finish helps protect against normal household wear, scratches, and scuffs, supporting long-lasting residential performance in active spaces.

The layered core construction also contributes to a more stable floor under seasonal humidity fluctuations, helping maintain the integrity and appearance of the installation over time.

Its balanced combination of durability and authentic hardwood visuals makes this collection especially appealing for homeowners seeking a premium wood floor that feels both stylish and practical.

#### Product Information

###### Product Overview

### Tungston Unfinished White Oak Solid Hardwood Flooring

Create a truly custom hardwood floor with Tungston Unfinished White Oak Solid Hardwood Flooring. Made from premium solid white oak, this unfinished flooring gives you complete control over the final look of your space. Whether you prefer a natural clear finish, a rich traditional stain, or a modern custom color, these planks provide the perfect foundation for a floor tailored to your style and budget.

Known for its enduring popularity, white oak remains one of the most sought-after hardwood species thanks to its balance of strength, durability, and classic beauty. The natural grain patterns and versatile appearance make it an ideal choice for both traditional and contemporary interiors.

#### Create a Fully Customized Hardwood Floor

This unfinished solid hardwood flooring is designed for projects where customization matters. Because the planks arrive unfinished, they can be sanded, stained, and finished on-site to achieve virtually any desired color and sheen level.

Available in multiple widths and grading options, the collection allows homeowners, designers, and builders to create everything from clean, refined spaces to character-rich rustic interiors. The unfinished surface ensures a personalized result that cannot be replicated with prefinished flooring.

#### Built for Lasting Performance

White oak has been a trusted flooring choice in North America for generations because of its durability and timeless appeal. Solid hardwood construction provides long-term value and can be refinished multiple times throughout its lifespan.

A standout feature of the Tungston collection is its extra-long plank format. Through specialized manufacturing and harvesting techniques, planks can reach lengths up to 120 inches, creating a more seamless, high-end appearance with fewer visible end joints. Backed by a lifetime structural warranty, this flooring is designed for decades of dependable performance.

#### Professional Installation Experience

The traditional tongue-and-groove milling system helps ensure a secure fit between boards during installation. Designed for nail-down or staple-down installation methods, this flooring delivers the authentic feel and stability expected from solid hardwood.

Once installed, the floor can be sanded and finished according to the specific design vision of the project, allowing for complete customization from surface texture to stain color.

#### Where This Product Works Best

- Living rooms and family rooms
- Dining rooms
- Bedrooms
- Hallways and entryways
- Custom home construction
- Historic home renovations
- Luxury residential projects
- Light commercial environments
- Projects requiring custom stain and finish selections

- Gloss Level: Natural
- Shade: Medium/Neutral
- Applications: Residential and Commercial
- Radiant Heat Compatible: No
- Structural Warranty: Lifetime

#### Why This Is a Good Choice

For customers who want complete creative freedom, Tungston Unfinished White Oak Solid Hardwood Flooring offers the ideal combination of customization and long-term durability. The unfinished surface allows for limitless design possibilities, while the solid white oak construction delivers the authentic beauty and performance that have made oak flooring a favorite for over a century. Extra-long planks, multiple width options, and a lifetime structural warranty further enhance its value for custom flooring projects.

###### Benefits

At BuildDirect, our moldings are designed as a blended match, meaning they are car

- Unfinished Hardwood Moldings - White Oak

Share:

#### Product Information

###### Product Overview

Flooring moldings are essential finishing components that complete a flooring installation. They create smooth transitions, cover expansion gaps, and provide clean edges where flooring meets walls, doorways, stairs, or adjacent surfaces.

At BuildDirect, our moldings are designed as a blended match, meaning they are carefully crafted to complement the color, tone, and finish of your flooring rather than replicate it exactly. Because flooring materials naturally vary in grain, texture, and color, subtle differences between moldings and floor boards are expected and are not considered defects.

To achieve the best visual result, installers may sort flooring boards prior to installation and pre-select boards that will sit next to moldings or transitions. This allows the natural variation in the flooring to work with the molding for a cohesive, high-quality finish.

Some moldings may be produced using different materials or species than the flooring itself. Even so, they are designed to visually complement the flooring as closely as possible while meeting performance and durability requirements.

Most flooring moldings are made to order after purchase. Production time is factored into estimated shipping timelines, and flooring should not be installed until moldings have arrived to ensure proper fit and finish.

The right molding doesn’t just finish a floor - it helps protect the installation, improve longevity, and deliver a polished result that holds up over time.

###### Benefits

Creates Clean, Professional Transitions: Flooring moldings help connect rooms and surfaces smoothly, reducing visible edges and creating a finished, intentional look throughout the
space.

Protects Floor Edges: By covering exposed edges and expansion gaps, moldings help reduce chipping, cracking, and wear in high-traffic areas.

Accommodates Natural Floor Movement: Many flooring products expand and contract over time. Properly installed moldings help allow for this movement without compromising
performance or appearance.

Enhances Safety in Key Areas: Stair nosing and transition moldings help reduce trip hazards and improve stability where floor heights change.

Completes the Overall Design: Blended-match moldings are designed to visually complement flooring, helping tie the room together while allowing for natural variation in
color and grain.

Compatible Across Flooring Types: Suitable for hardwood, laminate, vinyl, and engineered flooring, moldings support consistent finishes across mixed materials and room layouts.

REAL Bamboo and REAL waterproof flooring that you can depend on.

When only the natural beauty and warmth of real bamboo will do, the 100% waterproof strand bamboo with rigid core provides flooring solutions for the home or office. Guaranteed to outperform traditional engineered bamboo flooring, this 100% waterproof strand bamboo couples the performance virtues of rigid core flooring with the unique “character” of real strand bamboo and none of the repeat patterns found in vinyl or laminate.

The increased strength and density of the rigid core that hides sub-floor imperfections combined with a click locking system which floats over most existing hard surface floors is a perfect solution for the DIY homeowner. Backed by a lifetime limited residential warranty, the waterproof strand bamboo is CARB 2 certified and EPA (TSCA title VI) compliant. It also has the coveted GREENGUARD Gold certification. Waterproof, the added benefit of a safer product, and the tough Aluminum Oxide finish provides the right flooring solution for active families or the office.

###### Product Overview

##### Plush Carpet in a Box – 24" x 40" - Serenity Collection

Bring cozy comfort and modern convenience together with the Serenity Collection Plush Carpet in a Box. Designed for homeowners who want a soft, inviting floor without the complexity of traditional carpet installs, this boxed carpet solution delivers a clean, finished look in a manageable format that’s easy to transport, install, and enjoy.

This plush carpet offers a smooth, uniform surface underfoot, making it ideal for creating warm, relaxing spaces throughout the home. Its neutral styling blends effortlessly with both contemporary and classic interiors.

##### Made for Simple, Flexible Use

This carpet-in-a-box format is ideal for room refreshes, smaller spaces, and projects where ease of handling matters. Whether you’re updating a bedroom, finishing a basement, or adding softness to a home office, the pre-sized design helps reduce waste and simplify planning.

##### Comfort You Can Rely On

The plush pile construction delivers consistent softness and a cushioned feel that holds up to everyday living. Designed for residential use, this carpet provides a balance of comfort and durability suitable for low- to moderate-traffic areas.

The dense fiber structure helps maintain a smooth appearance while offering insulation and sound absorption benefits that hard flooring can’t match.

##### Easy Installation Experience

Installation is quick and straightforward using the FriXion Pad attachment system. This system securely holds the carpet in place without permanent adhesives, making installation faster, cleaner, and more flexible than traditional methods.

Because the tiles are not permanently bonded, individual pieces can be lifted, repositioned, or replaced without disrupting the entire floor, ideal for DIY projects and evolving spaces.

##### Easy to Clean & Maintain

Designed with real-life living in mind, these carpet tiles are machine washable up to three times. This makes them especially well suited for homes with kids, pets, or high spill risk, allowing you to refresh your flooring without professional cleaning or replacement.

##### Ideal Rooms & Spaces

- Bedrooms
- Living rooms
- Home offices
- Basements
- Guest rooms
- Apartments and rental properties

If you’re looking for a soft, welcoming carpet that’s easy to install, easy to maintain, and simple to replace, this Serenity Collection option is a smart choice. The combination of plush comfort, removable installation, and machine-washable convenience makes it especially appealing for modern households and DIY-friendly projects.

#### Product Information

###### Product Overview

### Composite Decking Deck Clips

These composite decking deck clips are designed to create a clean, professional-looking deck surface while securely locking boards in place. Engineered for compatibility with grooved composite decking, they eliminate visible screws and help deliver a smooth, uninterrupted finish that homeowners and contractors prefer.

Each package includes everything needed to fasten approximately 50 square feet of decking, making it easy to plan materials accurately for your project.

#### Clean, Hidden Fastening for Composite Deck Boards

Deck clips are used to fasten grooved composite decking boards to the substructure without face-screwing. They fit into the side grooves of each board, maintaining consistent spacing while holding boards firmly in place as the deck expands and contracts with temperature changes.

This system includes both standard T-clips for field installation and locking clips to secure starting or ending boards, ensuring a stable, uniform deck layout from edge to edge.

#### Built for Outdoor Durability

Manufactured from corrosion-resistant materials, these deck clips are built to withstand moisture, temperature fluctuations, and everyday wear. They provide dependable holding power while allowing controlled board movement, helping reduce stress on fasteners and extending the life of your deck system.

Their low-profile design supports proper drainage and airflow, contributing to a more stable and durable deck structure over time.

#### Installer-Friendly Design

Deck clips are designed for straightforward installation with standard tools. The included mix of clips supports efficient installation while maintaining consistent spacing across the deck surface. Whether you’re a professional installer or a confident DIYer, these clips help reduce installation time and deliver clean, repeatable results.

They are commonly installed following the fastening guidelines recommended by most composite decking manufacturers.

#### Where This Product Works Best

- Residential composite deck installations
- Outdoor decks, patios, and walkways
- Projects requiring hidden fasteners
- Grooved composite decking boards
- New builds and deck replacements

#### Quick Specs Summary

- Coverage per package: 50 sq. ft.
- Includes: 75 CEC Clips (T-Clips) and 15 Locking Clips
- Designed for grooved composite decking
- Hidden fastening system (no surface screws)
- Maintains consistent board spacing
- Corrosion-resistant construction

#### Why This Is a Good Choice

If you’re aiming for a clean, modern deck appearance without exposed fasteners, these composite decking deck clips are a smart solution. With pre-measured coverage per package and the right mix of standard and locking clips, they simplify planning and installation while supporting long-term deck performance.

###### Benefits

- Creates a smooth, fastener-free deck surface
- Helps maintain consistent spacing between boards
- Supports natural expansion and contraction
- Simplifies material planning with 50 sq. ft. coverage per pack
- Improves overall deck appearance
- Streamlines composite deck installation

### Durashield® Decking Touch-Up Paint for Quick Surface Repairs

Keep your deck looking clean and consistent with Durashield® Decking Touch-Up Paint. Designed to fix small scratches, scuffs, and exposed edges, this easy-to-use solution helps preserve the finished look of your Durashield decking after installation and everyday use.

It’s the simple way to blend away minor imperfections and keep boards looking uniform, without replacing materials.

#### Designed for Small Deck Repairs and Finishing Touches

This touch-up paint is used to correct light cosmetic damage on composite deck boards. It’s ideal for covering cut ends, surface scratches, and small nicks that may occur during installation or regular wear. Apply directly to the affected area to quickly improve the board’s appearance.

#### Built to Match and Hold Up Outdoors

Specially formulated for Durashield composite decking, this paint adheres well to composite surfaces and dries to a durable, natural-looking finish. Once cured, it helps disguise imperfections while standing up to typical outdoor conditions.

This product is intended for appearance-focused repairs, not structural fixes.

#### Easy Application with Minimal Prep

Whether you’re a DIY homeowner or a professional installer, application is fast and straightforward. No special tools are required, and most touch-ups can be completed in just minutes. For best results, apply to clean, dry surfaces and allow proper drying time before walking on the area.

#### Ideal Uses Around Your Deck

- Covering light scratches and scuffs on composite boards
- Touching up exposed cut edges after installation
- Fixing minor marks from furniture or foot traffic
- Final detailing before project completion

#### Product Details at a Glance

- Cosmetic repair solution
- Helps conceal scratches, scuffs, and cut ends
- Simple, direct application
- Intended for exterior deck surfaces

#### A Smart Way to Maintain a Finished Look

Replacing boards for small cosmetic flaws can be expensive and unnecessary. Durashield Decking Touch-Up Paint offers a cost-effective way to maintain a clean, professional appearance with minimal effort—making it a practical addition to any deck installation or maintenance kit.

- Restores a clean, uniform decking appearance
- Blends with Durashield decking colors
- Ideal for quick, small-area repairs
- Simple application with minimal prep

### Polished Travertine Tile – Premium Natural Stone Tile for Elegant Floors and Walls

Enhance your home with the timeless elegance of Polished Travertine Tile. Crafted from genuine natural travertine, this luxurious stone tile features a smooth, polished finish that highlights the stone's unique veining and warm, natural color variations. Ideal for floors, walls, backsplashes, bathrooms, and commercial spaces, polished travertine delivers a sophisticated look that never goes out of style.

#### Refined Natural Beauty for Every Room

Polished Travertine Tile showcases the distinctive character of natural stone with a glossy finish that reflects light and adds depth to any space. Its subtle earth tones and organic patterns create an inviting atmosphere that complements traditional, transitional, and contemporary interiors. Whether you're designing a spa-inspired bathroom, an elegant foyer, or a statement kitchen backsplash, this premium tile offers lasting visual appeal.

#### Durable Natural Stone Performance

Travertine has been used in architecture for centuries thanks to its durability and timeless beauty. The polished surface enhances the stone's natural elegance while providing a smooth, refined finish suitable for residential and light commercial applications. With proper sealing and routine care, polished travertine maintains its beauty and performance for years to come.

#### Professional Installation for Lasting Results

Designed for mortar-set installation over properly prepared substrates, Polished Travertine Tile is suitable for a wide variety of interior applications. Natural stone installations benefit from professional installation and periodic sealing to help protect against moisture, staining, and everyday wear. Once installed, the polished surface is easy to maintain with regular cleaning using stone-safe products.

###### Product Overview

### Blue Stone French Pattern Set Marble Tile

Bring timeless European style to your home with the Blue Stone French Pattern Set Marble Tile. Crafted from premium-grade natural marble, this elegant flooring features beautiful gray-blue tones, a sandblasted and brushed surface, and softly rounded edges that create the authentic look of aged stone. The classic Versailles pattern adds depth, movement, and old-world charm to both traditional and contemporary spaces, while the natural variation in every tile ensures a truly one-of-a-kind installation.

#### Designed for Timeless Living Spaces

This French pattern marble tile is ideal for homeowners and designers looking to create a sophisticated natural stone floor with lasting character. Its textured finish offers a refined appearance while helping to soften the look of everyday wear, making it an excellent choice for:

- Kitchens
- Bathrooms
- Entryways
- Living and dining rooms
- Sunrooms
- Covered patios
- Courtyards
- Luxury residential renovations

The versatile gray and blue color palette complements wood cabinetry, natural finishes, modern fixtures, and classic architectural styles alike.

#### Premium Natural Stone Performance

Manufactured from carefully selected premium-grade marble, each tile showcases unique veining and color variation that cannot be replicated by manufactured materials. The sandblasted and brushed finish enhances the stone's natural texture while providing a warm, inviting appearance. At ½-inch thick, these marble tiles deliver the durability and substantial feel expected from high-quality natural stone flooring.

#### Installation Made for Authentic Results

Each French Pattern Set includes four coordinating tile sizes that interlock to create the classic Versailles layout. The modular pattern produces an elegant, custom appearance with minimal repetition.

For the most authentic installation, these tiles are designed to be installed with tight butt joints and little to no visible grout lines, creating the seamless look traditionally found in Mediterranean estates and European villas. Professional installation is recommended for the best pattern alignment and long-term performance.

Elevate any space with the timeless beauty of Turkish Carrara White Polished Marble Tile. Quarried from premium natural marble, this elegant tile features a crisp white background accented by distinctive gray veining, creating a luxurious look that never goes out of style. Its polished finish enhances the depth and character of the stone, reflecting light to brighten interiors while delivering the sophisticated appearance associated with classic Carrara marble.

#### Bring Luxury to Floors, Walls, and Feature Spaces

Designed for both traditional and contemporary interiors, Turkish Carrara White Polished Marble Tile is a versatile choice for homeowners, designers, and contractors seeking a refined natural stone finish. Its clean, neutral palette complements virtually any color scheme and works beautifully in:

- Kitchens
- Bathrooms

- Accent walls
- Living and dining rooms
- Residential and light commercial interiors

Whether used as flooring or a decorative wall surface, polished marble creates an upscale, timeless aesthetic that enhances property value and design appeal.

#### Exceptional Natural Stone Beauty

Crafted from dense Turkish marble, each tile showcases unique veining, subtle color variation, and natural mineral characteristics that make every installation one of a kind. The polished surface delivers an exceptionally smooth, glossy finish that highlights the stone's natural movement while reflecting light to create brighter, more open spaces.

As a genuine natural stone product, slight variations in color, pattern, and veining are expected and celebrated as part of marble's authentic character.

#### Professional Installation & Care

The precision-cut tiles provide a clean, elegant installation with narrow grout joints for a seamless appearance. Because polished marble is a natural stone, sealing before use and periodic resealing are recommended to help protect against staining and simplify maintenance.

For installation, unsanded grout is generally recommended for polished marble to help prevent scratching the finished surface. Regular cleaning with a pH-neutral stone cleaner will help preserve its natural beauty for years to come.

#### Where This Product Works Best

- Kitchen floors and backsplashes
- Shower surrounds
- Entryways and foyers

- Residential and light commercial projects

> FloorMuffler 3030 kraft paper underlayment — breathable Class II vapor retarder for nail-down hardwood, engineered & laminate floors. 500 sq ft per roll.

- FloorMuffler 3030 Kraft Paper Underlayment & Vapor Retarder – 500 sq. ft. Roll

Share:

#### Product Information

###### Product Overview

#### FloorMuffler 3030 Underlayment Paper – Breathable Moisture Barrier for Hardwood Floors

FloorMuffler 3030 is an engineered kraft paper underlayment that protects wood flooring from subfloor moisture the smart way — by slowing vapor without trapping it. As a breathable Class II vapor retarder, it shields your floor from moisture migrating up through the subfloor while still letting the assembly dry. It's the modern upgrade to asphalt felt and rosin paper, without the mess or the odor.

##### Built for Nail-Down Hardwood

This is the underlayment pros reach for under nail-down solid hardwood. Roll it out over the plywood or OSB subfloor, overlap the seams, and nail your floor right through it. The slip sheet reduces squeaks from board-on-subfloor friction and keeps dust and debris out of the assembly — clean, fast, and code-friendly.

##### One Roll, Most Installs

3030 isn't limited to nail-down. Use it under floating engineered hardwood and laminate as a vapor retarder, and — unlike foam underlayments — it's fully compatible with flooring that has a pre-attached pad. When your plank already has cushion built in, 3030 adds the moisture protection layer without doubling up on foam (which can void flooring warranties).

##### Pro-Grade Coverage

Each roll covers 500 square feet — enough to knock out most whole-floor jobs with one SKU. At 36 inches wide, it lays out fast with fewer seams than narrow felt rolls, cuts cleanly with a utility knife, and weighs a fraction of asphalt paper.

Where it works best:

- Nail-down solid hardwood over plywood or OSB
- Floating engineered hardwood and laminate floors
- Flooring with pre-attached underlayment pad (moisture layer only)
- Whole-home and multi-room residential or commercial projects

Good to know: Not for use with radiant heat systems. Over concrete slabs or in basements, use an underlayment with a full moisture barrier such as our FanFold XPS Flooring Underlayment.

Backed by a 25-year residential and commercial warranty.

###### Benefits

- Enhances comfort underfoot
- Helps reduce footfall noise
- Protects against subfloor moisture
- Smooths minor subfloor imperfections
- Easy roll-out installation
- Suitable for laminate and engineered hardwood floors

#### FloorMuffler ComboFoam 2-in-1 Underlayment with Attached Vapor Barrier

Two jobs, one roll, one low price. FloorMuffler ComboFoam combines a 2mm cushioning foam with an attached vapor barrier film, so you get underlayment and moisture protection in a single pass — no separate poly sheeting, no extra step. It's the budget-smart foundation for floating laminate, engineered hardwood, and solid hardwood floors.

##### Vapor Barrier Built In

The attached film layer blocks moisture migrating up from the subfloor — the protection laminate and wood floors need over concrete slabs and in vapor-prone areas. Roll it out, tape the seams, and the moisture layer is done.

##### Real Sound Numbers at a Value Price

ComboFoam carries a 61 dB IIC and 54 dB STC rating — genuine, tested acoustic performance that takes the hollow clack out of floating floors. For bedrooms, living areas, rentals, and flips, that's the comfort upgrade tenants and buyers notice, at a price that works across a whole property.

##### Made for Volume Work

At 100 square feet per roll and 46.5 inches wide, ComboFoam covers rooms fast with fewer seams. It's lightweight, lies flat, cuts with a utility knife, and won't slow down a crew turning multiple units. For landlords, flippers, and production work, it's the spec that keeps material cost per door predictable.

##### From the FloorMuffler Family

ComboFoam is made by FloorMuffler, the underlayment brand pros know — backed by a 10-year residential warranty.

Where it works best:

- Floating laminate, engineered, and solid hardwood floors
- Concrete and plywood subfloors
- Rental turnovers, flips, and budget remodels

#### Product Information

###### Product Overview

#### FloorMuffler UltraSeal Underlayment – Premium Sound & Moisture Protection

When the job calls for the quietest floor and a sealed moisture barrier, this is the underlayment pros spec by name. FloorMuffler UltraSeal is a premium cross-linked polypropylene (XLPP) foam underlayment with some of the highest acoustic ratings available — up to 74 dB IIC, 73 dB STC, and a 25 dB Delta IIC — under laminate, engineered hardwood, and solid hardwood floors.

##### Pass the Sound Test

Condos, apartments, and HOAs often require flooring assemblies to meet minimum IIC/STC ratings. UltraSeal's ratings clear the strictest building requirements, making it the go-to choice for multi-family projects, upper floors, and anywhere footstep noise is a dealbreaker. If a board needs to approve your floor, this is the spec sheet you want attached.

##### Sealed Seams, Continuous Moisture Barrier

The signature UltraSeal lip-and-tape system is built into every roll: each row overlaps and self-seals to the next, creating one continuous moisture barrier across the entire floor — no separate vapor film, no taping every seam by hand. Over concrete slabs, that sealed barrier is what protects your floor's locking system and your warranty.

##### Premium Foam That Holds Up

XLPP foam delivers superior compression resistance, supporting the tongue-and-groove locking system of floating floors so joints stay tight for the life of the floor. At 2mm, it smooths minor subfloor imperfections and adds cushion underfoot without the sponginess that damages click-lock joints.

##### Versatile Across Installs

Use it under floating laminate, floating or nail-down engineered hardwood, and nail-down solid hardwood, over concrete or wood subfloors. Compatible with electric and hydronic radiant heat.

Where it works best:

- Condos, apartments, and multi-family projects with sound requirements
- Floating laminate and engineered hardwood floors
- Nail-down solid and engineered hardwood
- Concrete slabs and basements needing a sealed moisture barrier
- Radiant heat installations

Not for use with: flooring that has a pre-attached pad.

Backed by FloorMuffler's limited lifetime residential warranty and 10-year commercial warranty.

###### Benefits

- Helps protect floors from subfloor moisture
- Improves comfort and reduces footfall noise
- Smooths minor subfloor imperfections
- Quick and easy roll-out installation

#### Product Information

###### Product Overview

#### FloorMuffler Encore Underlayment for Vinyl Plank & Rigid Core Flooring

Most underlayments aren't made for today's vinyl floors. FloorMuffler Encore is. This premium 1.1mm XLPP underlayment is purpose-built for luxury vinyl plank and rigid core flooring (SPC and WPC) — including planks that already have a pre-attached pad. Where standard foam is too thick and too soft for vinyl click systems, Encore's low-profile, high-density construction supports the locking mechanism instead of stressing it.

##### Yes — It Works With Pre-Attached Pad

Have rigid core flooring with the pad already attached? Encore is one of the few underlayments engineered to go underneath it, adding sound reduction and moisture protection without compromising the locking system or your flooring warranty. That makes it the answer to one of the most common questions in vinyl flooring — "can I add underlayment under attached-pad LVP?" With Encore: yes.

##### Built-In Moisture Barrier

The closed-cell XLPP core is itself a moisture barrier — no separate vapor film required. Over concrete slabs and in basements, that's the layer standing between subfloor moisture and your floor.

##### Quieter Vinyl Floors

Rigid core floors can sound hollow and clicky underfoot. Encore delivers up to 69 dB IIC, 62 dB STC, and a 22 dB Delta IIC, cutting impact noise and that hollow sound — and helping vinyl installs clear condo and HOA sound requirements.

##### Protects the Click, Smooths the Sub

At 1.1mm, Encore stays within the thin, firm profile vinyl manufacturers call for, with outstanding compression strength that keeps tongue-and-groove joints tight under traffic. It smooths minor subfloor imperfections, adds an R-4.0/inch thermal layer, and is compatible with electric and hydronic radiant heat.

Where it works best:

- Luxury vinyl plank (LVP) and luxury vinyl tile (LVT) floating floors
- SPC and WPC rigid core flooring — with or without attached pad
- Condos, apartments, and rentals where sound matters
- Concrete slabs, basements, and plywood subfloors
- Radiant heat installations

Backed by FloorMuffler's limited lifetime residential warranty and 10-year commercial warranty.

###### Benefits

- Improves sound absorption and noise reduction
- Enhances underfoot comfort
- Supports long-term floor performance
- Easy to install and cut to size
- Suitable for residential and light commercial use

Designed for quick installation and long-term outdoor durability, these tiles deliver the look of decking with the flexibility of a modular system.

#### A Simple Way to Transform Outdoor Surfaces

These composite deck tiles are designed to float over existing surfaces like concrete, stone, or waterproof membranes. Each tile locks together securely, creating a stable, finished surface that can be installed or removed as needed, perfect for renters, seasonal spaces, or quick outdoor upgrades.

#### Built for Outdoor Performance

Manufactured by JF Outdoor, these composite tiles resist moisture, fading, and everyday outdoor wear. The composite construction won’t splinter or rot, making them a low-maintenance alternative to traditional wood decking in exposed environments.

#### Easy, Tool-Free Installation

Installation is straightforward and fast. The interlocking base system allows tiles to snap together by hand, eliminating the need for adhesives, fasteners, or specialized tools. Tiles can be trimmed at edges for a clean fit and lifted later for drainage access or layout changes.

#### Ideal Applications

- Balconies and terraces
- Patios and poolside areas
- Rooftop decks
- Walkways and garden paths
- Temporary or rental outdoor spaces

#### Quick Specs

- Composite surface with plastic base
- Designed for exterior use

#### Product Information

###### Product Overview

### QuickDeck HD Composite Interlocking Deck Tiles

Refresh patios, balconies, and rooftop spaces in a single afternoon with QuickDeck® HD Composite Interlocking Deck Tiles. Designed for speed, durability, and modern outdoor style, these tiles deliver the look of composite decking without permanent construction, heavy tools, or ongoing maintenance. It’s a clean, flexible solution for upgrading outdoor surfaces fast.

Whether you’re covering aging concrete or creating a new outdoor living area, QuickDeck HD offers a polished finish that installs right over existing hard surfaces.

#### An Easy Way to Upgrade Outdoor Spaces Without Construction

QuickDeck HD tiles function as a floating outdoor floor system that snaps together securely above the surface below. There’s no need for adhesives, screws, or substructure framing, making them ideal for quick renovations, rentals, and spaces where permanent changes aren’t an option.

#### Built to Handle Weather, Moisture, and Everyday Use

These tiles are made with durable composite decking material designed for outdoor exposure. They resist moisture, temperature swings, and surface wear better than traditional wood. The raised base promotes airflow and drainage, helping water escape quickly after rain and reducing the risk of pooling or surface damage.

#### Snap-Together Installation That’s DIY-Friendly

Each tile clicks into place by hand, allowing entire areas to come together quickly and evenly. Tiles can be trimmed with standard tools for clean edges around walls, posts, and railings. Because the system isn’t permanently fixed, tiles can be lifted and reinstalled later if access beneath the surface is needed.

#### Ideal for These Outdoor Areas

- Condo balconies and apartment patios
- Rooftop decks and terraces
- Concrete patios and walkways
- Poolside seating areas
- Temporary or renter-friendly outdoor upgrades

#### Quick Specs at a Glance

- Product Type: Composite interlocking deck tiles
- Installation Style: Floating snap-lock system
- Surface Material: Wood-plastic composite
- Base Design: Raised backing for drainage and airflow
- Maintenance Level: Low; no sealing or staining required

#### A Smart Choice for Fast, Flexible Outdoor Flooring

QuickDeck HD is ideal for customers who want the durability and appearance of composite decking without the cost, complexity, or permanence of traditional deck builds. Its quick installation, low maintenance, and removable design make it especially well suited for urban living, retrofits, and short renovation timelines.

###### Benefits

- Fast snap-together installation with no fasteners
- Composite surface resists moisture, warping, and splintering
- Raised design allows for proper drainage
- Perfect for patios, balconies, and rooftops
- Low-maintenance alternative to traditional decking
- Tiles can be removed or replaced individually

Bring the warmth of natural wood to your outdoor space without the ongoing maintenance. Dura-Shield Ultratex Composite Decking offers rich colour variation, realistic wood grain texture, and dependable durability designed for everyday outdoor living. It’s a long-lasting alternative to traditional lumber that won’t rot, warp, or splinter over time.

#### Designed for Beautiful Outdoor Living

Ultratex composite decking boards are used to create strong, comfortable walking surfaces for decks, patios, and outdoor platforms. Whether you’re building a new deck or replacing aging boards, this composite solution delivers a clean, finished look that elevates any outdoor space.

#### Built to Last with Long-Term Protection

Engineered with advanced composite technology, Dura-Shield Ultratex decking is built to withstand moisture, temperature fluctuations, and daily foot traffic. The durable outer layer helps resist fading and staining, keeping your deck looking great year after year. For added peace of mind, this decking is backed by a 25-year residential warranty, making it a reliable choice for long-term outdoor projects.

#### Easy Installation with Practical Design Flexibility

These boards install using standard decking tools and hardware, making them suitable for professional installers and confident DIYers alike. Available in both 8-foot and 16-foot lengths, Ultratex decking allows for flexible layout options with fewer seams on larger decks. Select profiles also feature a reversible surface, giving you the choice between a smooth finish or a wood-grain texture.

#### Ideal Applications

- Backyard and garden decks
- Patios and outdoor entertaining areas
- Poolside surrounds
- Cottages and vacation properties
- Raised platforms, stairs, and walkways

- Moisture-resistant and low maintenance
- No sealing, staining, or sanding required
- Multiple colour and profile options available

#### A Smart Alternative to Traditional Wood

Dura-Shield Ultratex Composite Decking delivers the look of natural wood with the advantages of modern composite performance. With consistent colour, minimal upkeep, and a 25-year residential warranty, it’s a smart investment for homeowners who want lasting value and dependable outdoor performance. Offered by BuildDirect, this decking is designed to meet the demands of real outdoor living.

###### Benefits

- Realistic wood-look finish with rich colour variation
- Durable composite construction resists moisture and decay
- Low-maintenance surface with no sealing or staining
- Available in 8' and 16' board lengths for layout flexibility
- Backed by a 25-year residential warranty

At BuildDirect, our moldings are designed as a blended match, meaning they are car

- Home
- Laminate Moldings - 12mm Wide Board Collection - Hickory Antique

Share:

#### Product Information

###### Product Overview

Flooring moldings are essential finishing components that complete a flooring installation. They create smooth transitions, cover expansion gaps, and provide clean edges where flooring meets walls, doorways, stairs, or adjacent surfaces.

At BuildDirect, our moldings are designed as a blended match, meaning they are carefully crafted to complement the color, tone, and finish of your flooring rather than replicate it exactly. Because flooring materials naturally vary in grain, texture, and color, subtle differences between moldings and floor boards are expected and are not considered defects.

To achieve the best visual result, installers may sort flooring boards prior to installation and pre-select boards that will sit next to moldings or transitions. This allows the natural variation in the flooring to work with the molding for a cohesive, high-quality finish.

Some moldings may be produced using different materials or species than the flooring itself. Even so, they are designed to visually complement the flooring as closely as possible while meeting performance and durability requirements.

Most flooring moldings are made to order after purchase. Production time is factored into estimated shipping timelines, and flooring should not be installed until moldings have arrived to ensure proper fit and finish.

The right molding doesn’t just finish a floor - it helps protect the installation, improve longevity, and deliver a polished result that holds up over time.

###### Benefits

Creates Clean, Professional Transitions: Flooring moldings help connect rooms and surfaces smoothly, reducing visible edges and creating a finished, intentional look throughout the
space.

Protects Floor Edges: By covering exposed edges and expansion gaps, moldings help reduce chipping, cracking, and wear in high-traffic areas.

Accommodates Natural Floor Movement: Many flooring products expand and contract over time. Properly installed moldings help allow for this movement without compromising
performance or appearance.

Enhances Safety in Key Areas: Stair nosing and transition moldings help reduce trip hazards and improve stability where floor heights change.

Completes the Overall Design: Blended-match moldings are designed to visually complement flooring, helping tie the room together while allowing for natural variation in
color and grain.

Compatible Across Flooring Types: Suitable for hardwood, laminate, vinyl, and engineered flooring, moldings support consistent finishes across mixed materials and room layouts.

#### Product Information

###### Product Overview

Flooring moldings are essential finishing components that complete a flooring installation. They create smooth transitions, cover expansion gaps, and provide clean edges where flooring meets walls, doorways, stairs, or adjacent surfaces.

At BuildDirect, our moldings are designed as a blended match, meaning they are carefully crafted to complement the color, tone, and finish of your flooring rather than replicate it exactly. Because flooring materials naturally vary in grain, texture, and color, subtle differences between moldings and floor boards are expected and are not considered defects.

To achieve the best visual result, installers may sort flooring boards prior to installation and pre-select boards that will sit next to moldings or transitions. This allows the natural variation in the flooring to work with the molding for a cohesive, high-quality finish.

Some moldings may be produced using different materials or species than the flooring itself. Even so, they are designed to visually complement the flooring as closely as possible while meeting performance and durability requirements.

Most flooring moldings are made to order after purchase. Production time is factored into estimated shipping timelines, and flooring should not be installed until moldings have arrived to ensure proper fit and finish.

The right molding doesn’t just finish a floor - it helps protect the installation, improve longevity, and deliver a polished result that holds up over time.

###### Benefits

Creates Clean, Professional Transitions: Flooring moldings help connect rooms and surfaces smoothly, reducing visible edges and creating a finished, intentional look throughout the
space.

Protects Floor Edges: By covering exposed edges and expansion gaps, moldings help reduce chipping, cracking, and wear in high-traffic areas.

Accommodates Natural Floor Movement: Many flooring products expand and contract over time. Properly installed moldings help allow for this movement without compromising
performance or appearance.

Enhances Safety in Key Areas: Stair nosing and transition moldings help reduce trip hazards and improve stability where floor heights change.

Completes the Overall Design: Blended-match moldings are designed to visually complement flooring, helping tie the room together while allowing for natural variation in
color and grain.

Compatible Across Flooring Types: Suitable for hardwood, laminate, vinyl, and engineered flooring, moldings support consistent finishes across mixed materials and room layouts.

Flooring moldings are essential finishing components that complete a flooring installation. They create smooth transitions, cover expansion gaps, and provide clean edges where flooring meets walls, doorways, stairs, or adjacent surfaces.

At BuildDirect, our moldings are designed as a blended match, meaning they are carefully crafted to complement the color, tone, and finish of your flooring rather than replicate it exactly. Because flooring materials naturally vary in grain, texture, and color, subtle differences between moldings and floor boards are expected and are not considered defects.

To achieve the best visual result, installers may sort flooring boards prior to installation and pre-select boards that will sit next to moldings or transitions. This allows the natural variation in the flooring to work with the molding for a cohesive, high-quality finish.

Some moldings may be produced using different materials or species than the flooring itself. Even so, they are designed to visually complement the flooring as closely as possible while meeting performance and durability requirements.

Most flooring moldings are made to order after purchase. Production time is factored into estimated shipping timelines, and flooring should not be installed until moldings have arrived to ensure proper fit and finish.

The right molding doesn’t just finish a floor - it helps protect the installation, improve longevity, and deliver a polished result that holds up over time.

###### Benefits

Creates Clean, Professional Transitions: Flooring moldings help connect rooms and surfaces smoothly, reducing visible edges and creating a finished, intentional look throughout the
space.

Protects Floor Edges: By covering exposed edges and expansion gaps, moldings help reduce chipping, cracking, and wear in high-traffic areas.

Accommodates Natural Floor Movement: Many flooring products expand and contract over time. Properly installed moldings help allow for this movement without compromising
performance or appearance.

Enhances Safety in Key Areas: Stair nosing and transition moldings help reduce trip hazards and improve stability where floor heights change.

Completes the Overall Design: Blended-match moldings are designed to visually complement flooring, helping tie the room together while allowing for natural variation in
color and grain.

Compatible Across Flooring Types: Suitable for hardwood, laminate, vinyl, and engineered flooring, moldings support consistent finishes across mixed materials and room layouts.

#### Product Information

###### Product Overview

Flooring moldings are essential finishing components that complete a flooring installation. They create smooth transitions, cover expansion gaps, and provide clean edges where flooring meets walls, doorways, stairs, or adjacent surfaces.

At BuildDirect, our moldings are designed as a blended match, meaning they are carefully crafted to complement the color, tone, and finish of your flooring rather than replicate it exactly. Because flooring materials naturally vary in grain, texture, and color, subtle differences between moldings and floor boards are expected and are not considered defects.

To achieve the best visual result, installers may sort flooring boards prior to installation and pre-select boards that will sit next to moldings or transitions. This allows the natural variation in the flooring to work with the molding for a cohesive, high-quality finish.

Some moldings may be produced using different materials or species than the flooring itself. Even so, they are designed to visually complement the flooring as closely as possible while meeting performance and durability requirements.

Most flooring moldings are made to order after purchase. Production time is factored into estimated shipping timelines, and flooring should not be installed until moldings have arrived to ensure proper fit and finish.

The right molding doesn’t just finish a floor - it helps protect the installation, improve longevity, and deliver a polished result that holds up over time.

###### Benefits

Creates Clean, Professional Transitions: Flooring moldings help connect rooms and surfaces smoothly, reducing visible edges and creating a finished, intentional look throughout the
space.

Protects Floor Edges: By covering exposed edges and expansion gaps, moldings help reduce chipping, cracking, and wear in high-traffic areas.

Accommodates Natural Floor Movement: Many flooring products expand and contract over time. Properly installed moldings help allow for this movement without compromising
performance or appearance.

Enhances Safety in Key Areas: Stair nosing and transition moldings help reduce trip hazards and improve stability where floor heights change.

Completes the Overall Design: Blended-match moldings are designed to visually complement flooring, helping tie the room together while allowing for natural variation in
color and grain.

Compatible Across Flooring Types: Suitable for hardwood, laminate, vinyl, and engineered flooring, moldings support consistent finishes across mixed materials and room layouts.

Share:

#### Product Information

###### Product Overview

Flooring moldings are essential finishing components that complete a flooring installation. They create smooth transitions, cover expansion gaps, and provide clean edges where flooring meets walls, doorways, stairs, or adjacent surfaces.

At BuildDirect, our moldings are designed as a blended match, meaning they are carefully crafted to complement the color, tone, and finish of your flooring rather than replicate it exactly. Because flooring materials naturally vary in grain, texture, and color, subtle differences between moldings and floor boards are expected and are not considered defects.

To achieve the best visual result, installers may sort flooring boards prior to installation and pre-select boards that will sit next to moldings or transitions. This allows the natural variation in the flooring to work with the molding for a cohesive, high-quality finish.

Some moldings may be produced using different materials or species than the flooring itself. Even so, they are designed to visually complement the flooring as closely as possible while meeting performance and durability requirements.

Most flooring moldings are made to order after purchase. Production time is factored into estimated shipping timelines, and flooring should not be installed until moldings have arrived to ensure proper fit and finish.

The right molding doesn’t just finish a floor - it helps protect the installation, improve longevity, and deliver a polished result that holds up over time.

###### Benefits

Creates Clean, Professional Transitions: Flooring moldings help connect rooms and surfaces smoothly, reducing visible edges and creating a finished, intentional look throughout the
space.

Protects Floor Edges: By covering exposed edges and expansion gaps, moldings help reduce chipping, cracking, and wear in high-traffic areas.

Accommodates Natural Floor Movement: Many flooring products expand and contract over time. Properly installed moldings help allow for this movement without compromising
performance or appearance.

Enhances Safety in Key Areas: Stair nosing and transition moldings help reduce trip hazards and improve stability where floor heights change.

Completes the Overall Design: Blended-match moldings are designed to visually complement flooring, helping tie the room together while allowing for natural variation in
color and grain.

Compatible Across Flooring Types: Suitable for hardwood, laminate, vinyl, and engineered flooring, moldings support consistent finishes across mixed materials and room layouts.

> Our moldings are not designed to be an exact match, they are carefully blended to complement the color and finish of your floor, therefore variation in wood grain and color in different pieces is to be expected. Please note this is not a defect but a way to capture the various natural grain and color characteristics of

Order up to 5 Free Samples Today

Take Our Quiz To Find Your Perfect Floor →

- Home
- Engineered Hardwood Moldings - LongHorn - Heritage

Share:

#### Product Information

###### Product Overview

Our moldings are not designed to be an exact match, they are carefully blended to complement the color and finish of your floor, therefore variation in wood grain and color in different pieces is to be expected. Please note this is not a defect but a way to capture the various natural grain and color characteristics of the flooring. Whether you want a uniform look or a variated look between your moldings and flooring, we suggest having the installer sort through floor boards prior to installation to select end boards that best pair with the look you are after. Pre-selecting flooring that will lay next to your moldings before installation will ensure an exceptional result that meets, or even exceeds, your expectations.

Please note that our moldings may be made from different materials or species than the floor boards, but are still crafted to complement any given flooring as closely as possible.

Please note that these products are made to order, only after the order is placed. This production time is taken into consideration when estimating shipping timelines, so please expect a longer lead time for the production of these custom made products. Also, as with all of our special order products that are custom made, our 30 Day Money Back Guarantee does not extend to these custom made products.

Flooring should not be installed until the moldings have arrived. Please click this video link that outlines the installation requirements and blending process.
